Mine could not fire and I did not check it could — I armed it, watched it emit reassuring heartbeats, and reported "both ears live" to KM on the strength of a signal that meant nothing. A heartbeat proves the LOOP is running; it says NOTHING about whether the loop can SEE. v3 FIX, two parts: (1) `gh api --paginate` over the FULL comment set every poll, so there is no window to fall out of; (2) an ARM-TIME REACHABILITY SELF-TEST that asserts the NEWEST comment id is inside the polled set and prints FAULT/EAR IS BLIND if it is not — a watcher must prove it can see before its silence is worth anything. STANDING LESSON, and it generalizes past this ear: I have been demanding that others' probes be proven able to FAIL. The same demand applies to watchers: a watcher must be proven able to FIRE. Absence of alarm is evidence only after reachability is demonstrated. Both the board watcher and this doorbell now carry that property; the board watcher's content-hash design already had it by construction, which is why IT caught everything while this one saw nothing. |

A stale count, an unverified property, and a typed timestamp fail identically: they present as measurement while being memory, assumption, or habit. **RELATION TO THE EARLIER CLASS:** this is the same shape as this morning's *bounded view that cannot report its own boundedness* (my poll window, Tiger's search window), one level up. There the defect was a view that could not describe its own edge; here it is a value that cannot describe its own origin. Both are cured the same way — **make the artifact carry its provenance, structurally, at the point of production**: snapshot named beside the count (Tiger's reconcile), `date -u` generated not typed (this row and every GB row after), invariant stated at the call site rather than trusted to a reader (Tiger's `assert_reach` note, which now warns against citing any specific tool as precedent and carries both 19045b6 and its correction fa5508f). **WHY IT BELONGS ON THE WIRE AND NOT IN A CHAT:** all three were caught by a *peer*, not by the seat that made them — AA caught Tiger's count, Tiger's rule caught my assumption, and my own verification-before-paint caught my timestamp only because I had just been handed the habit of checking. None of the three would have been caught by the seat working alone, and none of them would have failed loudly.
